+426|
+427|function applyMove(accountId, columnOneBased) {
+428|  const columnIndex = columnOneBased - 1;
+429|  const current = getGameRow(accountId);
+430|  const state = {
+431|    board: JSON.parse(current.board_json),
+432|    currentPlayer: current.current_player,
+433|    status: current.status,
+434|    winnerColor: current.winner_color,
+435|    winningCells: JSON.parse(current.winning_cells_json),
+436|    totals: {
+437|      redWins: current.red_wins,
+438|      yellowWins: current.yellow_wins,
+439|      draws: current.draws,
+440|    },
+441|  };
+442|
+443|  if (state.status !== 'active') {
+444|    return { ok: false, status: 409, message: 'Game is over. Start a new 
game.' };
+445|  }
+446|
+447|  if (!Number.isInteger(columnOneBased) || columnOneBased < 1 || 
columnOneBased > 7) {
+448|    return { ok: false, status: 400, message: 'Choose a column from 1 to 
7.' };
+449|  }
+450|
+451|  const dropped = dropInColumn(state.board, columnIndex, 
state.currentPlayer);
+452|  if (!dropped) {
+453|    return { ok: false, status: 409, message: `Column ${columnOneBased} is 
full` };
+454|  }
+455|
+456|  const nextBoard = dropped.board;
+457|  const winningCells = detectWinningCells(nextBoard, dropped.rowIndex, 
columnIndex, state.currentPlayer);
+458|  const nextMoveNumber = getMoveCount(accountId) + 1;
+459|
+460|  const insertMove = db.prepare(`
+461|    INSERT INTO moves (account_id, move_number, color, column_index, 
row_index, applied)
+462|    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1)
+463|  `);
+464|
+465|  const run = db.transaction(() => {
+466|    db.prepare('DELETE FROM moves WHERE account_id = ? AND applied = 
0').run(accountId);
+467|    insertMove.run(accountId, nextMoveNumber, state.currentPlayer, 
columnIndex, dropped.rowIndex);
+468|
+469|    if (winningCells.length === 4) {
+470|      const winnerKey = state.currentPlayer === 'Red' ? 'redWins' : 
'yellowWins';
+471|      state.totals[winnerKey] += 1;
+472|      db.prepare(`
+473|        UPDATE game_state
+474|        SET board_json = ?, current_player = ?, status = 'won', 
winner_color = ?, winning_cells_json = ?,
+475|            red_wins = ?, yellow_wins = ?, draws = ?, updated_at = 
CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
+476|        WHERE account_id = ?
+477|      `).run(
+478|        serializeBoard(nextBoard),
+479|        state.currentPlayer === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red',
+480|        state.currentPlayer,
+481|        serializeWinningCells(winningCells),
+482|        state.totals.redWins,
+483|        state.totals.yellowWins,
+484|        state.totals.draws,
+485|        accountId,
+486|      );
+487|      return;
+488|    }
+489|
+490|    if (boardIsFull(nextBoard)) {
+491|      state.totals.draws += 1;
+492|      db.prepare(`
+493|        UPDATE game_state
+494|        SET board_json = ?, current_player = ?, status = 'draw', 
winner_color = NULL, winning_cells_json = ?,
+495|            red_wins = ?, yellow_wins = ?, draws = ?, updated_at = 
CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
+496|        WHERE account_id = ?
+497|      `).run(
+498|        serializeBoard(nextBoard),
+499|        state.currentPlayer === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red',
+500|        serializeWinningCells([]),
+501|        state.totals.redWins,
+502|        state.totals.yellowWins,
+503|        state.totals.draws,
+504|        accountId,
+505|      );
+506|      return;
+507|    }
+508|
+509|    db.prepare(`
+510|      UPDATE game_state
+511|      SET board_json = ?, current_player = ?, status = 'active', 
winner_color = NULL, winning_cells_json = ?,
+512|          red_wins = ?, yellow_wins = ?, draws = ?, updated_at = 
CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
+513|      WHERE account_id = ?
+514|    `).run(
+515|      serializeBoard(nextBoard),
+516|      state.currentPlayer === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red',
+517|      serializeWinningCells([]),
+518|      state.totals.redWins,
+519|      state.totals.yellowWins,
+520|      state.totals.draws,
+521|      accountId,
+522|    );
+523|  });
+524|
+525|  run();
+526|  return { ok: true, feedback: '' };
+527|}
+528|
+529|function undoMove(accountId) {
+530|  const current = getGameRow(accountId);
+531|  const lastMove = db.prepare(`
+532|    SELECT * FROM moves
+533|    WHERE account_id = ? AND applied = 1
+534|    ORDER BY move_number DESC
+535|    LIMIT 1
+536|  `).get(accountId);
+537|
+538|  if (!lastMove) {
+539|    return { ok: false, status: 409, message: 'Nothing to undo' };
+540|  }
+541|
+542|  const state = {
+543|    board: JSON.parse(current.board_json),
+544|    currentPlayer: current.current_player,
+545|    status: current.status,
+546|    winnerColor: current.winner_color,
+547|    winningCells: JSON.parse(current.winning_cells_json),
+548|    totals: {
+549|      redWins: current.red_wins,
+550|      yellowWins: current.yellow_wins,
+551|      draws: current.draws,
+552|    },
+553|  };
+554|
+555|  const index = rowColToIndex(lastMove.row_index, lastMove.column_index);
+556|  state.board[index] = null;
+557|  state.currentPlayer = lastMove.color;
+558|  state.status = 'active';
+559|  state.winnerColor = null;
+560|  state.winningCells = [];
+561|  if (current.status === 'won') {
+562|    if (current.winner_color === 'Red') {
+563|      state.totals.redWins -= 1;
+564|    } else if (current.winner_color === 'Yellow') {
+565|      state.totals.yellowWins -= 1;
+566|    }
+567|  } else if (current.status === 'draw') {
+568|    state.totals.draws -= 1;
+569|  }
+570|
+571|  const run = db.transaction(() => {
+572|    db.prepare('UPDATE moves SET applied = 0 WHERE account_id = ? AND 
move_number = ?').run(accountId, lastMove.move_number);
+573|    db.prepare(`
+574|      UPDATE game_state
+575|      SET board_json = ?, current_player = ?, status = 'active', 
winner_color = NULL, winning_cells_json = ?,
+576|          red_wins = ?, yellow_wins = ?, draws = ?, updated_at = 
CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
+577|      WHERE account_id = ?
+578|    `).run(
+579|      serializeBoard(state.board),
+580|      state.currentPlayer,
+581|      serializeWinningCells([]),
+582|      state.totals.redWins,
+583|      state.totals.yellowWins,
+584|      state.totals.draws,
+585|      accountId,
+586|    );
+587|  });
+588|
+589|  run();
+590|  return { ok: true, feedback: '' };
+591|}
+592|
+593|function redoMove(accountId) {
+594|  const undoneMove = db.prepare(`
+595|    SELECT * FROM moves
+596|    WHERE account_id = ? AND applied = 0
+597|    ORDER BY move_number DESC
+598|    LIMIT 1
+599|  `).get(accountId);
+600|
+601|  if (!undoneMove) {
+602|    return { ok: false, status: 409, message: 'Nothing to redo' };
+603|  }
+604|
+605|  const current = getGameRow(accountId);
+606|  const state = {
+607|    board: JSON.parse(current.board_json),
+608|    currentPlayer: current.current_player,
+609|    status: current.status,
+610|    winnerColor: current.winner_color,
+611|    winningCells: JSON.parse(current.winning_cells_json),
+612|    totals: {
+613|      redWins: current.red_wins,
+614|      yellowWins: current.yellow_wins,
+615|      draws: current.draws,
+616|    },
+617|  };
+618|
+619|  if (state.status !== 'active') {
+620|    return { ok: false, status: 409, message: 'Game is over. Start a new 
game.' };
+621|  }
+622|
+623|  const index = rowColToIndex(undoneMove.row_index, 
undoneMove.column_index);
+624|  if (state.board[index] !== null) {
+625|    return { ok: false, status: 409, message: 'That move can no longer be 
redone.' };
+626|  }
+627|
+628|  state.board[index] = undoneMove.color;
+629|  const winningCells = detectWinningCells(state.board, 
undoneMove.row_index, undoneMove.column_index, undoneMove.color);
+630|
+631|  const run = db.transaction(() => {
+632|    db.prepare('UPDATE moves SET applied = 1 WHERE account_id = ? AND 
move_number = ?').run(accountId, undoneMove.move_number);
+633|
+634|    if (winningCells.length === 4) {
+635|      const winnerKey = undoneMove.color === 'Red' ? 'redWins' : 
'yellowWins';
+636|      state.totals[winnerKey] += 1;
+637|      db.prepare(`
+638|        UPDATE game_state
+639|        SET board_json = ?, current_player = ?, status = 'won', 
winner_color = ?, winning_cells_json = ?,
+640|            red_wins = ?, yellow_wins = ?, draws = ?, updated_at = 
CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
+641|        WHERE account_id = ?
+642|      `).run(
+643|        serializeBoard(state.board),
+644|        undoneMove.color === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red',
+645|        undoneMove.color,
+646|        serializeWinningCells(winningCells),
+647|        state.totals.redWins,
+648|        state.totals.yellowWins,
+649|        state.totals.draws,
+650|        accountId,
+651|      );
+652|      return;
+653|    }
+654|
+655|    if (boardIsFull(state.board)) {
+656|      state.totals.draws += 1;
+657|      db.prepare(`
+658|        UPDATE game_state
+659|        SET board_json = ?, current_player = ?, status = 'draw', 
winner_color = NULL, winning_cells_json = ?,
+660|            red_wins = ?, yellow_wins = ?, draws = ?, updated_at = 
CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
+661|        WHERE account_id = ?
+662|      `).run(
+663|        serializeBoard(state.board),
+664|        undoneMove.color === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red',
+665|        serializeWinningCells([]),
+666|        state.totals.redWins,
+667|        state.totals.yellowWins,
+668|        state.totals.draws,
+669|        accountId,
+670|      );
+671|      return;
+672|    }
+673|
+674|    db.prepare(`
+675|      UPDATE game_state
+676|      SET board_json = ?, current_player = ?, status = 'active', 
winner_color = NULL, winning_cells_json = ?,
+677|          red_wins = ?, yellow_wins = ?, draws = ?, updated_at = 
CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
+678|      WHERE account_id = ?
+679|    `).run(
+680|      serializeBoard(state.board),
+681|      undoneMove.color === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red',
+682|      serializeWinningCells([]),
+683|      state.totals.redWins,
+684|      state.totals.yellowWins,
+685|      state.totals.draws,
+686|      accountId,
+687|    );
+688|  });
+689|
+690|  run();
+691|  return { ok: true, feedback: '' };
+692|}

0').run(accountId);

+706|app.post('/api/login', (req, res) => {
+707|  const email = String(req.body?.email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
+708|  const password = String(req.body?.password || '');
+709|  const account = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM accounts WHERE lower(email) = 
?').get(email);
+710|  if (!account) {
+711|    return sendError(res, 401, 'Incorrect email or password.');
+712|  }
+713|  const computed = hashPassword(password, account.password_salt);
+714|  if (computed !== account.password_hash) {
+715|    return sendError(res, 401, 'Incorrect email or password.');
+716|  }
+717|
+718|  const token = createToken(account.id);
+719|  const payload = getSessionPayload(account.id);
+720|  res.json({ token, ...payload });
+721|});

+725|  if (!auth) {
+726|    return;
+727|  }
+728|  db.prepare('DELETE FROM tokens WHERE token_hash = 
?').run(sha256(auth.token));

+748|app.post('/api/move', (req, res) => {
+749|  const auth = requireAuth(req, res);
+750|  if (!auth) {
+751|    return;
+752|  }
+753|
+754|  const column = Number(req.body?.column);
+755|  const outcome = applyMove(auth.account.id, column);
+756|  if (!outcome.ok) {
+757|    return sendError(res, outcome.status, outcome.message);
+758|  }
+759|
+760|  res.json(getSessionPayload(auth.account.id));
+761|});
+762|
+763|app.post('/api/undo', (req, res) => {
+764|  const auth = requireAuth(req, res);
+765|  if (!auth) {
+766|    return;
+767|  }
+768|
+769|  const outcome = undoMove(auth.account.id);
+770|  if (!outcome.ok) {
+771|    return sendError(res, outcome.status, outcome.message);
+772|  }
+773|
+774|  res.json(getSessionPayload(auth.account.id));
+775|});
+776|
+777|app.post('/api/redo', (req, res) => {
+778|  const auth = requireAuth(req, res);
+779|  if (!auth) {
+780|    return;
+781|  }
+782|
+783|  const outcome = redoMove(auth.account.id);
+784|  if (!outcome.ok) {
+785|    return sendError(res, outcome.status, outcome.message);
+786|  }
+787|
+788|  res.json(getSessionPayload(auth.account.id));
+789|});

+799|  })();

+801|  res.json(getSessionPayload(auth.account.id, 'New game started'));
+802|});
+803|
+804|app.get('/', (_req, res) => {
+805|  res.sendFile(path.join(PUBLIC_DIR, 'index.html'));
+806|});
+807|
+808|app.use((err, _req, res, _next) => {
+809|  console.error(err);
+810|  res.status(500).json({ error: 'Unexpected server error.' });
+811|});
+812|
+813|app.listen(PORT, '0.0.0.0', () => {
+814|  console.log(`DropLine listening on 0.0.0.0:${PORT}`);
+815|});

    const state = {
      token: localStorage.getItem('droplineToken') || '',
      session: null,
      loading: false,
    };
    
    const authScreen = document.getElementById('authScreen');
    const gameScreen = document.getElementById('gameScreen');
    const loginForm = document.getElementById('loginForm');
    const emailInput = document.getElementById('emailInput');
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('passwordInput');
    const authFeedback = document.getElementById('authFeedback');
    const accountBadge = document.getElementById('accountBadge');
    const statusText = document.getElementById('statusText');
    const turnHint = document.getElementById('turnHint');
    const redWinsValue = document.getElementById('redWinsValue');
    const yellowWinsValue = document.getElementById('yellowWinsValue');
    const drawsValue = document.getElementById('drawsValue');
    const columnControls = document.getElementById('columnControls');
    const board = document.getElementById('board');
    const gameFeedback = document.getElementById('gameFeedback');
    const newGameButton = document.getElementById('newGameButton');
    const undoButton = document.getElementById('undoButton');
    const redoButton = document.getElementById('redoButton');
    const signOutButton = document.getElementById('signOutButton');
    const historyList = document.getElementById('historyList');
    const availabilityText = document.getElementById('availabilityText');
    
    let columnButtons = [];
    let boardCells = [];
    
    function setSignedOut() {
      state.session = null;
      authScreen.classList.remove('hidden');
      gameScreen.classList.add('hidden');
      accountBadge.hidden = true;
      setFeedback(authFeedback, '');
      setFeedback(gameFeedback, '');
      loginForm.reset();
      passwordInput.focus();
    }
    
    function setSignedIn(session) {
      state.session = session;
      authScreen.classList.add('hidden');
      gameScreen.classList.remove('hidden');
      accountBadge.hidden = false;
      accountBadge.textContent = `Signed in as ${session.account.name} · 
${session.account.email}`;
      renderSession(session);
    }
    
    function setFeedback(element, message) {
      element.textContent = message || '';
    }
    
    function ensureBoardCells() {
      if (boardCells.length) {
        return;
      }
      board.innerHTML = '';
      for (let index = 0; index < 42; index += 1) {
        const cell = document.createElement('div');
        cell.className = 'cell';
        cell.setAttribute('role', 'gridcell');
        cell.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Row 1, Column 1, empty');
        cell.dataset.state = 'empty';
        board.appendChild(cell);
        boardCells.push(cell);
      }
    }
    
    function ensureColumnControls() {
      if (columnButtons.length) {
        return;
      }
      columnControls.innerHTML = '';
      for (let column = 1; column <= 7; column += 1) {
        const button = document.createElement('button');
        button.type = 'button';
        button.textContent = `Drop in column ${column}`;
        button.dataset.column = String(column);
        button.addEventListener('click', () => dropColumn(column));
        button.addEventListener('keydown', handleColumnKeydown);
        columnControls.appendChild(button);
        columnButtons.push(button);
      }
    }
    
    function handleColumnKeydown(event) {
      const index = columnButtons.indexOf(event.currentTarget);
      if (index === -1) {
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        event.preventDefault();
        const nextIndex = Math.max(0, index - 1);
        columnButtons[nextIndex].focus();
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        event.preventDefault();
        const nextIndex = Math.min(columnButtons.length - 1, index + 1);
        columnButtons[nextIndex].focus();
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'Home') {
        event.preventDefault();
        columnButtons[0].focus();
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'End') {
        event.preventDefault();
        columnButtons[columnButtons.length - 1].focus();
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
        event.preventDefault();
        dropColumn(index + 1);
      }
    }
    
    function cellLabel(row, column, cellState, winning) {
      let label = `Row ${row}, Column ${column}, ${cellState}`;
      if (winning) {
        label += ', winning';
      }
      return label;
    }
    
    function renderBoard(session) {
      ensureBoardCells();
      const winningSet = new Set(session.winningCells || []);
      for (let index = 0; index < boardCells.length; index += 1) {
        const cell = boardCells[index];
        const row = Math.floor(index / 7) + 1;
        const column = (index % 7) + 1;
        const cellState = session.board[index] || 'empty';
        const winning = winningSet.has(index);
        cell.dataset.state = cellState;
        cell.dataset.winning = winning ? 'true' : 'false';
        cell.setAttribute('aria-label', cellLabel(row, column, cellState, 
winning));
      }
    }
    
    function renderHistory(history) {
      historyList.innerHTML = '';
      if (!history.length) {
        const empty = document.createElement('li');
        empty.textContent = 'No moves yet.';
        historyList.appendChild(empty);
        return;
      }
    
      for (const move of history) {
        const item = document.createElement('li');
        item.textContent = `${move.moveNumber}. ${move.color} — column 
${move.column}, row ${move.row}`;
        historyList.appendChild(item);
      }
    }
    
    function renderSession(session) {
      setFeedback(gameFeedback, session.feedback || '');
      statusText.textContent = session.statusText;
      turnHint.textContent = session.status === 'active'
        ? 'Drop a disc into any column.'
        : 'Use New game, Undo, or Redo to continue exploring the saved round.';
      redWinsValue.textContent = String(session.totals.redWins);
      yellowWinsValue.textContent = String(session.totals.yellowWins);
      drawsValue.textContent = String(session.totals.draws);
      renderBoard(session);
      renderHistory(session.history);
      undoButton.disabled = !session.canUndo;
      redoButton.disabled = !session.canRedo;
      newGameButton.disabled = false;
      signOutButton.disabled = false;
      setColumnControlsDisabled(session.status !== 'active');
      availabilityText.textContent = [
        session.canUndo ? 'Undo available' : 'Nothing to undo',
        session.canRedo ? 'Redo available' : 'Nothing to redo',
      ].join(' · ');
    }
    
    function setColumnControlsDisabled(disabled) {
      ensureColumnControls();
      for (const button of columnButtons) {
        button.disabled = disabled;
      }
    }
    
    async function request(path, options = {}) {
      const headers = new Headers(options.headers || {});
      if (state.token) {
        headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${state.token}`);
      }
      if (options.body && !headers.has('Content-Type')) {
        headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json');
      }
    
      const response = await fetch(path, {
        ...options,
        headers,
      });
    
      const text = await response.text();
      const payload = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = payload.error || 'Unexpected request failure.';
        const error = new Error(message);
        error.status = response.status;
        throw error;
      }
      return payload;
    }
    
    async function bootstrap() {
      ensureBoardCells();
      ensureColumnControls();
    
      if (!state.token) {
        setSignedOut();
        return;
      }
    
      try {
        const payload = await request('/api/bootstrap');
        state.token = payload.token || state.token;
        localStorage.setItem('droplineToken', state.token);
        setSignedIn(payload);
      } catch (error) {
        state.token = '';
        localStorage.removeItem('droplineToken');
        setSignedOut();
        setFeedback(authFeedback, 'Your session expired. Please sign in 
again.');
      }
    }
    
    async function submitLogin(event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      setFeedback(authFeedback, '');
      const email = emailInput.value.trim();
      const password = passwordInput.value;
      if (!email || !password) {
        setFeedback(authFeedback, 'Enter both email and password.');
        return;
      }
    
      loginForm.querySelector('button[type="submit"]').disabled = true;
      try {
        const payload = await request('/api/login', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
        });
        state.token = payload.token;
        localStorage.setItem('droplineToken', state.token);
        setSignedIn(payload);
      } catch (error) {
        setFeedback(authFeedback, error.message);
      } finally {
        loginForm.querySelector('button[type="submit"]').disabled = false;
      }
    }
    
    async function performAction(path) {
      if (state.loading) {
        return;
      }
      state.loading = true;
      setFeedback(gameFeedback, '');
      try {
        const payload = await request(path, { method: 'POST', body: '{}' });
        setSignedIn(payload);
      } catch (error) {
        setFeedback(gameFeedback, error.message);
      } finally {
        state.loading = false;
      }
    }
    
    function dropColumn(column) {
      performAction('/api/move', { column });
    }
    
    // Override performAction for payload-specific requests.
    const originalPerformAction = performAction;
    performAction = async function performActionWithBody(path, body = {}) {
      if (state.loading) {
        return;
      }
      state.loading = true;
      setFeedback(gameFeedback, '');
      try {
        const payload = await request(path, {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify(body),
        });
        setSignedIn(payload);
      } catch (error) {
        setFeedback(gameFeedback, error.message);
      } finally {
        state.loading = false;
      }
    };
    
    loginForm.addEventListener('submit', submitLogin);
    newGameButton.addEventListener('click', () => 
performAction('/api/new-game'));
    undoButton.addEventListener('click', () => performAction('/api/undo'));
    redoButton.addEventListener('click', () => performAction('/api/redo'));
    signOutButton.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      if (!state.token) {
        setSignedOut();
        return;
      }
      try {
        await request('/api/logout', { method: 'POST', body: '{}' });
      } catch {
        // Even if logout fails, clear the local token.
      } finally {
        state.token = '':
        localStorage.removeItem('droplineToken');
        setSignedOut();
      }
    });
    
    board.addEventListener('pointerdown', () => {
      board.blur?.();
    });
    
    ensureBoardCells();
    ensureColumnControls();
    bootstrap();

+1|const state = {
+2|  token: localStorage.getItem('droplineToken') || '',
+3|  session: null,
+4|  loading: false,
+5|};
+6|

+28|
+29|let columnButtons = [];
+30|let boardCells = [];
+31|
+32|function setSignedOut() {
+33|  state.session = null;
+34|  authScreen.classList.remove('hidden');
+35|  gameScreen.classList.add('hidden');
+36|  accountBadge.hidden = true;
+37|  setFeedback(authFeedback, '');
+38|  setFeedback(gameFeedback, '');
+39|  loginForm.reset();
+40|  passwordInput.focus();
+41|}
+42|
+43|function setSignedIn(session) {
+44|  state.session = session;
+45|  authScreen.classList.add('hidden');
+46|  gameScreen.classList.remove('hidden');
+47|  accountBadge.hidden = false;
+48|  accountBadge.textContent = `Signed in as ${session.account.name} · 
${session.account.email}`;
+49|  renderSession(session);
+50|}
+51|
+52|function setFeedback(element, message) {
+53|  element.textContent = message || '';
+54|}
+55|
+56|function ensureBoardCells() {
+57|  if (boardCells.length) {
+58|    return;
+59|  }
+60|  board.innerHTML = '';
+61|  for (let index = 0; index < 42; index += 1) {
+62|    const cell = document.createElement('div');
+63|    cell.className = 'cell';
+64|    cell.setAttribute('role', 'gridcell');
+65|    cell.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Row 1, Column 1, empty');
+66|    cell.dataset.state = 'empty';
+67|    board.appendChild(cell);
+68|    boardCells.push(cell);
+69|  }
+70|}
+71|
+72|function ensureColumnControls() {
+73|  if (columnButtons.length) {
+74|    return;
+75|  }
+76|  columnControls.innerHTML = '';
+77|  for (let column = 1; column <= 7; column += 1) {
+78|    const button = document.createElement('button');
+79|    button.type = 'button';
+80|    button.textContent = `Drop in column ${column}`;
+81|    button.dataset.column = String(column);
+82|    button.addEventListener('click', () => dropColumn(column));
+83|    button.addEventListener('keydown', handleColumnKeydown);
+84|    columnControls.appendChild(button);
+85|    columnButtons.push(button);
+86|  }
+87|}
+88|
+89|function handleColumnKeydown(event) {
+90|  const index = columnButtons.indexOf(event.currentTarget);
+91|  if (index === -1) {
+92|    return;
+93|  }
+94|
+95|  if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
+96|    event.preventDefault();
+97|    const nextIndex = Math.max(0, index - 1);
+98|    columnButtons[nextIndex].focus();
+99|    return;
+100|  }
+101|
+102|  if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') {
+103|    event.preventDefault();
+104|    const nextIndex = Math.min(columnButtons.length - 1, index + 1);
+105|    columnButtons[nextIndex].focus();
+106|    return;
+107|  }
+108|
+109|  if (event.key === 'Home') {
+110|    event.preventDefault();
+111|    columnButtons[0].focus();
+112|    return;
+113|  }
+114|
+115|  if (event.key === 'End') {
+116|    event.preventDefault();
+117|    columnButtons[columnButtons.length - 1].focus();
+118|    return;
+119|  }
+120|
+121|  if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
+122|    event.preventDefault();
+123|    dropColumn(index + 1);
+124|  }
+125|}
+126|
+127|function cellLabel(row, column, cellState, winning) {
+128|  let label = `Row ${row}, Column ${column}, ${cellState}`;
+129|  if (winning) {
+130|    label += ', winning';
+131|  }
+132|  return label;
+133|}
+134|
+135|function renderBoard(session) {
+136|  ensureBoardCells();
+137|  const winningSet = new Set(session.winningCells || []);
+138|  for (let index = 0; index < boardCells.length; index += 1) {
+139|    const cell = boardCells[index];
+140|    const row = Math.floor(index / 7) + 1;
+141|    const column = (index % 7) + 1;
+142|    const cellState = session.board[index] || 'empty';
+143|    const winning = winningSet.has(index);

    23  const undoButton = document.getElementById('undoButton');
    24  const redoButton = document.getElementById('redoButton');

    26  const historyList = document.getElementById('historyList');

    28  
    29  let columnButtons = [];
    30  let boardCells = [];
    31  
    32  function setSignedOut() {
    33    state.session = null;
    34    authScreen.classList.remove('hidden');
    35    gameScreen.classList.add('hidden');
    36    accountBadge.hidden = true;
    37    setFeedback(authFeedback, '');
    38    setFeedback(gameFeedback, '');
    39    loginForm.reset();
    40    passwordInput.focus();
    41  }
    42  
    43  function setSignedIn(session) {
    44    state.session = session;
    45    authScreen.classList.add('hidden');
    46    gameScreen.classList.remove('hidden');
    47    accountBadge.hidden = false;
    48    accountBadge.textContent = `Signed in as ${session.account.name} · 
${session.account.email}`;
    49    renderSession(session);
    50  }
    51  
    52  function setFeedback(element, message) {
    53    element.textContent = message || '';
    54  }
    55  
    56  function ensureBoardCells() {
    57    if (boardCells.length) {
    58      return;
    59    }
    60    board.innerHTML = '';
    61    for (let index = 0; index < 42; index += 1) {
    62      const cell = document.createElement('div');
    63      cell.className = 'cell';
    64      cell.setAttribute('role', 'gridcell');
    65      cell.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Row 1, Column 1, empty');
    66      cell.dataset.state = 'empty';
    67      board.appendChild(cell);
    68      boardCells.push(cell);
    69    }
    70  }
    71  
    72  function ensureColumnControls() {
    73    if (columnButtons.length) {
    74      return;
    75    }
    76    columnControls.innerHTML = '';
    77    for (let column = 1; column <= 7; column += 1) {
    78      const button = document.createElement('button');
    79      button.type = 'button';
    80      button.textContent = `Drop in column ${column}`;
    81      button.dataset.column = String(column);
    82      button.addEventListener('click', () => dropColumn(column));
    83      button.addEventListener('keydown', handleColumnKeydown);
    84      columnControls.appendChild(button);
    85      columnButtons.push(button);
    86    }
    87  }
    88  
    89  function handleColumnKeydown(event) {
    90    const index = columnButtons.indexOf(event.currentTarget);
    91    if (index === -1) {
    92      return;
    93    }
    94  
    95    if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
    96      event.preventDefault();
    97      const nextIndex = Math.max(0, index - 1);
    98      columnButtons[nextIndex].focus();
    99      return;
   100    }
   101  
   102    if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') {
   103      event.preventDefault();
   104      const nextIndex = Math.min(columnButtons.length - 1, index + 1);
   105      columnButtons[nextIndex].focus();
   106      return;
   107    }
   108  
   109    if (event.key === 'Home') {
   110      event.preventDefault();
   111      columnButtons[0].focus();
   112      return;
   113    }
   114  
   115    if (event.key === 'End') {
   116      event.preventDefault();
   117      columnButtons[columnButtons.length - 1].focus();
   118      return;
   119    }
   120  
   121    if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
   122      event.preventDefault();
   123      dropColumn(index + 1);
   124    }
   125  }
   126  
   127  function cellLabel(row, column, cellState, winning) {
   128    let label = `Row ${row}, Column ${column}, ${cellState}`;
   129    if (winning) {
   130      label += ', winning';
   131    }
   132    return label;
   133  }
   134  
   135  function renderBoard(session) {
   136    ensureBoardCells();
   137    const winningSet = new Set(session.winningCells || []);
   138    for (let index = 0; index < boardCells.length; index += 1) {
   139      const cell = boardCells[index];
   140      const row = Math.floor(index / 7) + 1;
   141      const column = (index % 7) + 1;
   142      const cellState = session.board[index] || 'empty';
   143      const winning = winningSet.has(index);
   144      cell.dataset.state = cellState;
   145      cell.dataset.winning = winning ? 'true' : 'false';
   146      cell.setAttribute('aria-label', cellLabel(row, column, cellState, 
winning));
   147    }
   148  }
   149  
   150  function renderHistory(history) {
   151    historyList.innerHTML = '';
   152    if (!history.length) {
   153      const empty = document.createElement('li');
   154      empty.textContent = 'No moves yet.';
   155      historyList.appendChild(empty);
   156      return;
   157    }
   158  
   159    for (const move of history) {
   160      const item = document.createElement('li');
   161      item.textContent = `${move.moveNumber}. ${move.color} — column 
${move.column}, row ${move.row}`;
   162      historyList.appendChild(item);
   163    }
   164  }
   165  
   166  function renderSession(session) {
   167    setFeedback(gameFeedback, session.feedback || '');
   168    statusText.textContent = session.statusText;
   169    turnHint.textContent = session.status === 'active'
   170      ? 'Drop a disc into any column.'
   171      : 'Use New game, Undo, or Redo to continue exploring the saved 
round.';
   172    redWinsValue.textContent = String(session.totals.redWins);
   173    yellowWinsValue.textContent = String(session.totals.yellowWins);
   174    drawsValue.textContent = String(session.totals.draws);
   175    renderBoard(session);
   176    renderHistory(session.history);
   177    undoButton.disabled = !session.canUndo;
   178    redoButton.disabled = !session.canRedo;
   179    newGameButton.disabled = false;
   180    signOutButton.disabled = false;
   181    setColumnControlsDisabled(session.status !== 'active');
   182    availabilityText.textContent = [
   183      session.canUndo ? 'Undo available' : 'Nothing to undo',
   184      session.canRedo ? 'Redo available' : 'Nothing to redo',
   185    ].join(' · ');
   186  }
   187  
   188  function setColumnControlsDisabled(disabled) {
   189    ensureColumnControls();
   190    for (const button of columnButtons) {
   191      button.disabled = disabled;
   192    }
   193  }
   194  
   195  async function request(path, options = {}) {
   196    const headers = new Headers(options.headers || {});
   197    if (state.token) {
   198      headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${state.token}`);
   199    }
   200    if (options.body && !headers.has('Content-Type')) {
   201      headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json');
   202    }
   203  
   204    const response = await fetch(path, {
   205      ...options,
   206      headers,
   207    });
   208  
   209    const text = await response.text();
   210    const payload = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
   211    if (!response.ok) {
   212      const message = payload.error || 'Unexpected request failure.';
   213      const error = new Error(message);
   214      error.status = response.status;
   215      throw error;
   216    }
   217    return payload;
   218  }
   219  
   220  async function bootstrap() {
   221    ensureBoardCells();
   222    ensureColumnControls();
   223  
   224    if (!state.token) {
   225      setSignedOut();
   226      return;
   227    }
   228  
   229    try {
   230      const payload = await request('/api/bootstrap');
   231      state.token = payload.token || state.token;
   232      localStorage.setItem('droplineToken', state.token);
   233      setSignedIn(payload);
   234    } catch (error) {
   235      state.token = '';
   236      localStorage.removeItem('droplineToken');
   237      setSignedOut();
   238      setFeedback(authFeedback, 'Your session expired. Please sign in 
again.');
   239    }
   240  }
   241  
   242  async function submitLogin(event) {
   243    event.preventDefault();
   244    setFeedback(authFeedback, '');
   245    const email = emailInput.value.trim();
   246    const password = passwordInput.value;
   247    if (!email || !password) {
   248      setFeedback(authFeedback, 'Enter both email and password.');
   249      return;
   250    }
   251  
   252    loginForm.querySelector('button[type="submit"]').disabled = true;
   253    try {
   254      const payload = await request('/api/login', {
   255        method: 'POST',
   256        body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
   257      });
   258      state.token = payload.token;
   259      localStorage.setItem('droplineToken', state.token);
   260      setSignedIn(payload);
   261    } catch (error) {
   262      setFeedback(authFeedback, error.message);
   263    } finally {
   264      loginForm.querySelector('button[type="submit"]').disabled = false;
   265    }
   266  }
   267  
   268  async function performAction(path) {
   269    if (state.loading) {
   270      return;
   271    }
   272    state.loading = true;
   273    setFeedback(gameFeedback, '');
   274    try {
   275      const payload = await request(path, { method: 'POST', body: '{}' });
   276      setSignedIn(payload);
   277    } catch (error) {
   278      setFeedback(gameFeedback, error.message);
   279    } finally {
   280      state.loading = false;
   281    }
   282  }
   283  
   284  function dropColumn(column) {
   285    performAction('/api/move', { column });
   286  }
   287  
   288  // Override performAction for payload-specific requests.
   289  const originalPerformAction = performAction;
   290  performAction = async function performActionWithBody(path, body = {}) {
   291    if (state.loading) {
   292      return;
   293    }
   294    state.loading = true;
   295    setFeedback(gameFeedback, '');
   296    try {
   297      const payload = await request(path, {
   298        method: 'POST',
   299        body: JSON.stringify(body),
   300      });
   301      setSignedIn(payload);
   302    } catch (error) {
   303      setFeedback(gameFeedback, error.message);
   304    } finally {
   305      state.loading = false;
   306    }
   307  };
   308  
   309  loginForm.addEventListener('submit', submitLogin);
   310  newGameButton.addEventListener('click', () => 
performAction('/api/new-game'));
   311  undoButton.addEventListener('click', () => performAction('/api/undo'));
   312  redoButton.addEventListener('click', () => performAction('/api/redo'));
   313  signOutButton.addEventListener('click', async () => {
   314    if (!state.token) {
   315      setSignedOut();
   316      return;
   317    }
   318    try {
   319      await request('/api/logout', { method: 'POST', body: '{}' });
   320    } catch {
   321      // Even if logout fails, clear the local token.
   322    } finally {
   323      state.token = '':
   324      localStorage.removeItem('droplineToken');
   325      setSignedOut();
   326    }
   327  });

   330    board.blur?.();
   331  });

   335  bootstrap();

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "str_replace"

  old_str: 
    const state = {
      token: localStorage.getItem('droplineToken') || '',
      session: null,
      loading: false,
    };
    
    const authScreen = document.getElementById('authScreen');
    const gameScreen = document.getElementById('gameScreen');
    const loginForm = document.getElementById('loginForm');
    const emailInput = document.getElementById('emailInput');
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('passwordInput');
    const authFeedback = document.getElementById('authFeedback');
    const accountBadge = document.getElementById('accountBadge');
    const statusText = document.getElementById('statusText');
    const turnHint = document.getElementById('turnHint');
    const redWinsValue = document.getElementById('redWinsValue');
    const yellowWinsValue = document.getElementById('yellowWinsValue');
    const drawsValue = document.getElementById('drawsValue');
    const columnControls = document.getElementById('columnControls');
    const board = document.getElementById('board');
    const gameFeedback = document.getElementById('gameFeedback');
    const newGameButton = document.getElementById('newGameButton');
    const undoButton = document.getElementById('undoButton');
    const redoButton = document.getElementById('redoButton');
    const signOutButton = document.getElementById('signOutButton');
    const historyList = document.getElementById('historyList');
    const availabilityText = document.getElementById('availabilityText');
    
    let columnButtons = [];
    let boardCells = [];
    
    function setSignedOut() {
      state.session = null;
      authScreen.classList.remove('hidden');
      gameScreen.classList.add('hidden');
      accountBadge.hidden = true;
      setFeedback(authFeedback, '');
      setFeedback(gameFeedback, '');
      loginForm.reset();
      passwordInput.focus();
    }
    
    function setSignedIn(session) {
      state.session = session;
      authScreen.classList.add('hidden');
      gameScreen.classList.remove('hidden');
      accountBadge.hidden = false;
      accountBadge.textContent = `Signed in as ${session.account.name} · 
${session.account.email}`;
      renderSession(session);
    }
    
    function setFeedback(element, message) {
      element.textContent = message || '';
    }
    
    function ensureBoardCells() {
      if (boardCells.length) {
        return;
      }
      board.innerHTML = '';
      for (let index = 0; index < 42; index += 1) {
        const cell = document.createElement('div');
        cell.className = 'cell';
        cell.setAttribute('role', 'gridcell');
        cell.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Row 1, Column 1, empty');
        cell.dataset.state = 'empty';
        board.appendChild(cell);
        boardCells.push(cell);
      }
    }
    
    function ensureColumnControls() {
      if (columnButtons.length) {
        return;
      }
      columnControls.innerHTML = '';
      for (let column = 1; column <= 7; column += 1) {
        const button = document.createElement('button');
        button.type = 'button';
        button.textContent = `Drop in column ${column}`;
        button.dataset.column = String(column);
        button.addEventListener('click', () => dropColumn(column));
        button.addEventListener('keydown', handleColumnKeydown);
        columnControls.appendChild(button);
        columnButtons.push(button);
      }
    }
    
    function handleColumnKeydown(event) {
      const index = columnButtons.indexOf(event.currentTarget);
      if (index === -1) {
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        event.preventDefault();
        const nextIndex = Math.max(0, index - 1);
        columnButtons[nextIndex].focus();
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        event.preventDefault();
        const nextIndex = Math.min(columnButtons.length - 1, index + 1);
        columnButtons[nextIndex].focus();
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'Home') {
        event.preventDefault();
        columnButtons[0].focus();
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'End') {
        event.preventDefault();
        columnButtons[columnButtons.length - 1].focus();
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
        event.preventDefault();
        dropColumn(index + 1);
      }
    }
    
    function cellLabel(row, column, cellState, winning) {
      let label = `Row ${row}, Column ${column}, ${cellState}`;
      if (winning) {
        label += ', winning';
      }
      return label;
    }
    
    function renderBoard(session) {
      ensureBoardCells();
      const winningSet = new Set(session.winningCells || []);
      for (let index = 0; index < boardCells.length; index += 1) {
        const cell = boardCells[index];
        const row = Math.floor(index / 7) + 1;
        const column = (index % 7) + 1;
        const cellState = session.board[index] || 'empty';
        const winning = winningSet.has(index);
        cell.dataset.state = cellState;
        cell.dataset.winning = winning ? 'true' : 'false';
        cell.setAttribute('aria-label', cellLabel(row, column, cellState, 
winning));
      }
    }
    
    function renderHistory(history) {
      historyList.innerHTML = '';
      if (!history.length) {
        const empty = document.createElement('li');
        empty.textContent = 'No moves yet.';
        historyList.appendChild(empty);
        return;
      }
    
      for (const move of history) {
        const item = document.createElement('li');
        item.textContent = `${move.moveNumber}. ${move.color} — column 
${move.column}, row ${move.row}`;
        historyList.appendChild(item);
      }
    }
    
    function renderSession(session) {
      setFeedback(gameFeedback, session.feedback || '');
      statusText.textContent = session.statusText;
      turnHint.textContent = session.status === 'active'
        ? 'Drop a disc into any column.'
        : 'Use New game, Undo, or Redo to continue exploring the saved round.';
      redWinsValue.textContent = String(session.totals.redWins);
      yellowWinsValue.textContent = String(session.totals.yellowWins);
      drawsValue.textContent = String(session.totals.draws);
      renderBoard(session);
      renderHistory(session.history);
      undoButton.disabled = !session.canUndo;
      redoButton.disabled = !session.canRedo;
      newGameButton.disabled = false;
      signOutButton.disabled = false;
      setColumnControlsDisabled(session.status !== 'active');
      availabilityText.textContent = [
        session.canUndo ? 'Undo available' : 'Nothing to undo',
        session.canRedo ? 'Redo available' : 'Nothing to redo',
      ].join(' · ');
    }
    
    function setColumnControlsDisabled(disabled) {
      ensureColumnControls();
      for (const button of columnButtons) {
        button.disabled = disabled;
      }
    }
    
    async function request(path, options = {}) {
      const headers = new Headers(options.headers || {});
      if (state.token) {
        headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${state.token}`);
      }
      if (options.body && !headers.has('Content-Type')) {
        headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json');
      }
    
      const response = await fetch(path, {
        ...options,
        headers,
      });
    
      const text = await response.text();
      const payload = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = payload.error || 'Unexpected request failure.';
        const error = new Error(message);
        error.status = response.status;
        throw error;
      }
      return payload;
    }
    
    async function bootstrap() {
      ensureBoardCells();
      ensureColumnControls();
    
      if (!state.token) {
        setSignedOut();
        return;
      }
    
      try {
        const payload = await request('/api/bootstrap');
        state.token = payload.token || state.token;
        localStorage.setItem('droplineToken', state.token);
        setSignedIn(payload);
      } catch {
        state.token = '';
        localStorage.removeItem('droplineToken');
        setSignedOut();
        setFeedback(authFeedback, 'Your session expired. Please sign in 
again.');
      }
    }
    
    async function submitLogin(event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      setFeedback(authFeedback, '');
      const email = emailInput.value.trim();
      const password = passwordInput.value;
      if (!email || !password) {
        setFeedback(authFeedback, 'Enter both email and password.');
        return;
      }
    
      const submitButton = loginForm.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
      submitButton.disabled = true;
      try {
        const payload = await request('/api/login', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
        });
        state.token = payload.token;
        localStorage.setItem('droplineToken', state.token);
        setSignedIn(payload);
      } catch (error) {
        setFeedback(authFeedback, error.message);
      } finally {
        submitButton.disabled = false;
      }
    }
    
    async function performAction(path, body = {}) {
      if (state.loading) {
        return;
      }
      state.loading = true;
      setFeedback(gameFeedback, '');
      try {
        const payload = await request(path, {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify(body),
        });
        setSignedIn(payload);
      } catch (error) {
        setFeedback(gameFeedback, error.message);
      } finally {
        state.loading = false;
      }
    }
    
    function dropColumn(column) {
      performAction('/api/move', { column });
    }
    
    loginForm.addEventListener('submit', submitLogin);
    newGameButton.addEventListener('click', () => 
performAction('/api/new-game'));
    undoButton.addEventListener('click', () => performAction('/api/undo'));
    redoButton.addEventListener('click', () => performAction('/api/redo'));
    signOutButton.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      if (state.loading) {
        return;
      }
      try {
        await request('/api/logout', { method: 'POST', body: '{}' });
      } catch {
        // Clear the local token even if the server already revoked it.
      } finally {
        state.token = '';
        localStorage.removeItem('droplineToken');
        setSignedOut();
      }
    });
    
    ensureBoardCells();
    ensureColumnControls();
    bootstrap();
    

  new_str: 
    const state = {

    };
    
    const authScreen = document.getElementById('authScreen');
    const gameScreen = document.getElementById('gameScreen');
    const loginForm = document.getElementById('loginForm');
    const emailInput = document.getElementById('emailInput');
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('passwordInput');
    const authFeedback = document.getElementById('authFeedback');
    const accountBadge = document.getElementById('accountBadge');
    const statusText = document.getElementById('statusText');
    const turnHint = document.getElementById('turnHint');
    const redWinsValue = document.getElementById('redWinsValue');
    const yellowWinsValue = document.getElementById('yellowWinsValue');
    const drawsValue = document.getElementById('drawsValue');
    const columnControls = document.getElementById('columnControls');
    const board = document.getElementById('board');
    const gameFeedback = document.getElementById('gameFeedback');
    const newGameButton = document.getElementById('newGameButton');
    const undoButton = document.getElementById('undoButton');
    const redoButton = document.getElementById('redoButton');
    const signOutButton = document.getElementById('signOutButton');
    const historyList = document.getElementById('historyList');
    const availabilityText = document.getElementById('availabilityText');
    
    let columnButtons = [];
    let boardCells = [];
    
    function setFeedback(element, message) {
      element.textContent = message || '';
    }
    
    function ensureBoardCells() {
      if (boardCells.length) {
        return;
      }
      board.innerHTML = '';
      for (let index = 0; index < 42; index += 1) {
        const cell = document.createElement('div');
        cell.className = 'cell';
        cell.setAttribute('role', 'gridcell');
        cell.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Row 1, Column 1, empty');
        cell.dataset.state = 'empty';
        board.appendChild(cell);
        boardCells.push(cell);
      }
    }
    
    function ensureColumnControls() {
      if (columnButtons.length) {
        return;
      }
      columnControls.innerHTML = '';
      for (let column = 1; column <= 7; column += 1) {
        const button = document.createElement('button');
        button.type = 'button';
        button.textContent = `Drop in column ${column}`;
        button.dataset.column = String(column);
        button.addEventListener('click', () => dropColumn(column));
        button.addEventListener('keydown', handleColumnKeydown);
        columnControls.appendChild(button);
        columnButtons.push(button);
      }
    }
    
    function handleColumnKeydown(event) {
      const index = columnButtons.indexOf(event.currentTarget);
      if (index === -1) {
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        event.preventDefault();
        columnButtons[Math.max(0, index - 1)].focus();
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        event.preventDefault();
        columnButtons[Math.min(columnButtons.length - 1, index + 1)].focus();
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'Home') {
        event.preventDefault();
        columnButtons[0].focus();
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'End') {
        event.preventDefault();
        columnButtons[columnButtons.length - 1].focus();
        return;
      }
    
      if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
        event.preventDefault();
        dropColumn(index + 1);
      }
    }
    
    function cellLabel(row, column, cellState, winning) {
      let label = `Row ${row}, Column ${column}, ${cellState}`;
      if (winning) {
        label += ', winning';
      }
      return label;
    }
    
    function renderBoard(session) {
      ensureBoardCells();
      const winningSet = new Set(session.winningCells || []);
      for (let index = 0; index < boardCells.length; index += 1) {
        const cell = boardCells[index];
        const row = Math.floor(index / 7) + 1;
        const column = (index % 7) + 1;
        const cellState = session.board[index] || 'empty';
        const winning = winningSet.has(index);
        cell.dataset.state = cellState;
        cell.dataset.winning = winning ? 'true' : 'false';
        cell.setAttribute('aria-label', cellLabel(row, column, cellState, 
winning));
      }
    }
    
    function renderHistory(history) {
      historyList.innerHTML = '';
      if (!history.length) {
        const empty = document.createElement('li');
        empty.textContent = 'No moves yet.';
        historyList.appendChild(empty);
        return;
      }
    
      for (const move of history) {
        const item = document.createElement('li');
        item.textContent = `${move.moveNumber}. ${move.color} — column 
${move.column}, row ${move.row}`;
        historyList.appendChild(item);
      }
    }
    
    function setColumnControlsDisabled(disabled) {
      ensureColumnControls();
      for (const button of columnButtons) {
        button.disabled = disabled;
      }
    }
    
    function setSignedOut() {
      authScreen.classList.remove('hidden');
      gameScreen.classList.add('hidden');
      accountBadge.hidden = true;
      setFeedback(authFeedback, '');
      setFeedback(gameFeedback, '');
      loginForm.reset();
      passwordInput.focus();
    }
    
    function setSignedIn(session) {
      authScreen.classList.add('hidden');
      gameScreen.classList.remove('hidden');
      accountBadge.hidden = false;
      accountBadge.textContent = `Signed in as ${session.account.name} · 
${session.account.email}`;
      renderSession(session);
    }
    
    function renderSession(session) {
      setFeedback(gameFeedback, session.feedback || '');
      statusText.textContent = session.statusText;
      turnHint.textContent = session.status === 'active'
        ? 'Drop a disc into any column.'
        : 'Use New game, Undo, or Redo to continue exploring the saved round.';
      redWinsValue.textContent = String(session.totals.redWins);
      yellowWinsValue.textContent = String(session.totals.yellowWins);
      drawsValue.textContent = String(session.totals.draws);
      renderBoard(session);
      renderHistory(session.history);
      undoButton.disabled = !session.canUndo;
      redoButton.disabled = !session.canRedo;
      newGameButton.disabled = false;
      signOutButton.disabled = false;
      setColumnControlsDisabled(session.status !== 'active');
      availabilityText.textContent = [
        session.canUndo ? 'Undo available' : 'Nothing to undo',
        session.canRedo ? 'Redo available' : 'Nothing to redo',
      ].join(' · ');
    }
    
    async function request(path, options = {}) {
      const headers = new Headers(options.headers || {});
      if (state.token) {
        headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${state.token}`);
      }
      if (options.body && !headers.has('Content-Type')) {
        headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json');
      }
    
      const response = await fetch(path, {
        ...options,
        headers,
      });
    
      const text = await response.text();
      const payload = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = payload.error || 'Unexpected request failure.';
        const error = new Error(message);
        error.status = response.status;
        throw error;
      }
      return payload;
    }
    
    async function bootstrap() {
      ensureBoardCells();
      ensureColumnControls();
    
      if (!state.token) {
        setSignedOut();
        return;
      }
    
      try {
        const payload = await request('/api/bootstrap');
        state.token = payload.token || state.token;
        localStorage.setItem('droplineToken', state.token);
        setSignedIn(payload);
      } catch {
        state.token = '';
        localStorage.removeItem('droplineToken');
        setSignedOut();
        setFeedback(authFeedback, 'Your session expired. Please sign in 
again.');
      }
    }
    
    async function submitLogin(event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      setFeedback(authFeedback, '');
      const email = emailInput.value.trim();
      const password = passwordInput.value;
      if (!email || !password) {
        setFeedback(authFeedback, 'Enter both email and password.');
        return;
      }
    
      const submitButton = loginForm.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
      submitButton.disabled = true;
      try {
        const payload = await request('/api/login', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
        });
        state.token = payload.token;
        localStorage.setItem('droplineToken', state.token);
        setSignedIn(payload);
      } catch (error) {
        setFeedback(authFeedback, error.message);
      } finally {
        submitButton.disabled = false;
      }
    }
    
    async function performAction(path, body = {}) {
      if (state.loading) {
        return;
      }
      state.loading = true;
      setFeedback(gameFeedback, '');
      try {
        const payload = await request(path, {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify(body),
        });
        setSignedIn(payload);
      } catch (error) {
        setFeedback(gameFeedback, error.message);
      } finally {
        state.loading = false;
      }
    }
    
    function dropColumn(column) {
      performAction('/api/move', { column });
    }
    
    loginForm.addEventListener('submit', submitLogin);
    newGameButton.addEventListener('click', () => 
performAction('/api/new-game'));
    undoButton.addEventListener('click', () => performAction('/api/undo'));
    redoButton.addEventListener('click', () => performAction('/api/redo'));
    signOutButton.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      if (state.loading) {
        return;
      }
      try {
        await request('/api/logout', { method: 'POST', body: '{}' });
      } catch {
        // Clear the local token even if the server already revoked it.
      } finally {
        state.token = '';
        localStorage.removeItem('droplineToken');
        setSignedOut();
      }
    });
    
    ensureBoardCells();
    ensureColumnControls();
    bootstrap();

function handleColumnKeydown(event) {
  const index = columnButtons.indexOf(event.currentTarget);
  if (index === -1) {
    return;
  }

  if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
    event.preventDefault();
    const nextIndex = Math.max(0, index - 1);
    columnButtons[nextIndex].focus();
    return;
  }

  if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') {
    event.preventDefault();
    const nextIndex = Math.min(columnButtons.length - 1, index + 1);
    columnButtons[nextIndex].focus();
    return;
  }

  if (event.key === 'Home') {
    event.preventDefault();
    columnButtons[0].focus();
    return;
  }

  if (event.key === 'End') {
    event.preventDefault();
    columnButtons[columnButtons.length - 1].focus();
    return;
  }

  if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
    event.preventDefault();
    dropColumn(index + 1);
  }
}

function cellLabel(row, column, cellState, winning) {
  let label = `Row ${row}, Column ${column}, ${cellState}`;
  if (winning) {
    label += ', winning';
  }
  return label;
}

function renderBoard(session) {
  ensureBoardCells();
  const winningSet = new Set(session.winningCells || []);
  for (let index = 0; index < boardCells.length; index += 1) {
    const cell = boardCells[index];
    const row = Math.floor(index / 7) + 1;
    const column = (index % 7) + 1;
    const cellState = session.board[index] || 'empty';

    cell.dataset.state = cellState;
    cell.dataset.winning = winning ? 'true' : 'false';
    cell.setAttribute('aria-label', cellLabel(row, column, cellState, winning));
  }
}

  historyList.innerHTML = '';
  if (!history.length) {

async function request(path, options = {}) {
  const headers = new Headers(options.headers || {});
  if (state.token) {
    headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${state.token}`);
  }
  if (options.body && !headers.has('Content-Type')) {
    headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json');
  }

  const response = await fetch(path, {
    ...options,
    headers,
  });

  const text = await response.text();
  const payload = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
  if (!response.ok) {
    const message = payload.error || 'Unexpected request failure.';
    const error = new Error(message);
    error.status = response.status;
    throw error;
  }
  return payload;
}

async function bootstrap() {
  ensureBoardCells();
  ensureColumnControls();

  if (!state.token) {
    setSignedOut();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const payload = await request('/api/bootstrap');
    state.token = payload.token || state.token;
    localStorage.setItem('droplineToken', state.token);
    setSignedIn(payload);
  } catch {
    state.token = '';
    localStorage.removeItem('droplineToken');
    setSignedOut();
    setFeedback(authFeedback, 'Your session expired. Please sign in again.');
  }
}

async function submitLogin(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  setFeedback(authFeedback, '');
  const email = emailInput.value.trim();
  const password = passwordInput.value;
  if (!email || !password) {
    setFeedback(authFeedback, 'Enter both email and password.');
    return;
  }

  const submitButton = loginForm.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
  submitButton.disabled = true;
  try {
    const payload = await request('/api/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
    });
    state.token = payload.token;
    localStorage.setItem('droplineToken', state.token);
    setSignedIn(payload);
  } catch (error) {
    setFeedback(authFeedback, error.message);
  } finally {
    submitButton.disabled = false;
  }
}

async function performAction(path, body = {}) {
  if (state.loading) {
    return;
  }
  state.loading = true;
  setFeedback(gameFeedback, '');
  try {
    const payload = await request(path, {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });
    setSignedIn(payload);
  } catch (error) {
    setFeedback(gameFeedback, error.message);
  } finally {
    state.loading = false;
  }
}

  performAction('/api/move', { column });
}

}

async function request(path, options = {}) {
  const headers = new Headers(options.headers || {});
  if (state.token) {
    headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${state.token}`);
  }
  if (options.body && !headers.has('Content-Type')) {
    headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json');
  }

  const response = await fetch(path, {
    ...options,
    headers,
  });

  const text = await response.text();
  const payload = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
  if (!response.ok) {
    const message = payload.error || 'Unexpected request failure.';
    const error = new Error(message);
    error.status = response.status;
    throw error;
  }
  return payload;
}

async function bootstrap() {
  ensureBoardCells();
  ensureColumnControls();

  if (!state.token) {
    setSignedOut();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const payload = await request('/api/bootstrap');
    state.token = payload.token || state.token;
    localStorage.setItem('droplineToken', state.token);
    setSignedIn(payload);
  } catch {
    state.token = '';
    localStorage.removeItem('droplineToken');
    setSignedOut();
    setFeedback(authFeedback, 'Your session expired. Please sign in again.');
  }
}

async function submitLogin(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  setFeedback(authFeedback, '');
  const email = emailInput.value.trim();
  const password = passwordInput.value;
  if (!email || !password) {
    setFeedback(authFeedback, 'Enter both email and password.');
    return;
  }

  const submitButton = loginForm.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
  submitButton.disabled = true;
  try {
    const payload = await request('/api/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
    });
    state.token = payload.token;
    localStorage.setItem('droplineToken', state.token);
    setSignedIn(payload);
  } catch (error) {
    setFeedback(authFeedback, error.message);
  } finally {
    submitButton.disabled = false;
  }
}

async function performAction(path, body = {}) {
  if (state.loading) {
    return;
  }
  state.loading = true;
  setFeedback(gameFeedback, '');
  try {
    const payload = await request(path, {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });
    setSignedIn(payload);
  } catch (error) {
    setFeedback(gameFeedback, error.message);
  } finally {
    state.loading = false;
  }
}

function dropColumn(column) {
  performAction('/api/move', { column });
}

loginForm.addEventListener('submit', submitLogin);
newGameButton.addEventListener('click', () => performAction('/api/new-game'));
undoButton.addEventListener('click', () => performAction('/api/undo'));
redoButton.addEventListener('click', () => performAction('/api/redo'));
signOutButton.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  if (state.loading) {
    return;
  }
  try {
    await request('/api/logout', { method: 'POST', body: '{}' });
  } catch {
    // Clear the local token even if the server already revoked it.
  } finally {
    state.token = '';
    localStorage.removeItem('droplineToken');
    setSignedOut();
  }
});

ensureBoardCells();
ensureColumnControls();
bootstrap();

   326  function buildPayload(account, gameRow, moves, feedback = '') {
   327    const state = mapStateRow(gameRow);
   328    const appliedMoves = moves
   329      .filter((move) => move.applied === 1)
   330      .map((move) => ({
   331        moveNumber: move.move_number,
   332        color: move.color,
   333        column: move.column_index + 1,
   334        row: move.row_index + 1,
   335      }));
   336    const redoMoves = moves.filter((move) => move.applied === 0);
   337    const statusText = state.status === 'active'
   338      ? `${state.currentPlayer}'s turn`
   339      : state.status === 'won'
   340        ? `${state.winnerColor} wins`
   341        : 'Draw';
   342  
   343    return {
   344      account,
   345      board: state.board,
   346      currentPlayer: state.currentPlayer,
   347      status: state.status,
   348      statusText,
   349      winnerColor: state.winnerColor,
   350      winningCells: state.winningCells,
   351      totals: state.totals,
   352      history: appliedMoves,
   353      canUndo: appliedMoves.length > 0,
   354      canRedo: redoMoves.length > 0,
   355      feedback,
   356    };
   357  }
   358  
   359  function getSessionPayload(accountId, feedback = '') {
   360    const accountRow = db.prepare('SELECT id, email, name FROM accounts 
WHERE id = ?').get(accountId);
   361    const gameRow = getGameRow(accountId);
   362    const moves = getMoves(accountId);
   363    return buildPayload(
   364      { id: accountRow.id, email: accountRow.email, name: accountRow.name

   371  function createToken(accountId) {
   372    const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
   373    db.prepare('INSERT INTO tokens (token_hash, account_id) VALUES (?, 
?)').run(sha256(token), accountId);
   374    return token;
   375  }

   377  function getMoveCount(accountId) {
   378    const row = db.prepare('SELECT COALESCE(MAX(move_number), 0) AS 
max_move FROM moves WHERE account_id = ?').get(accountId);
   379    return row.max_move;

   382  function setGameState(accountId, updater) {
   383    const current = getGameRow(accountId);
   384    const next = updater({
   385      board: JSON.parse(current.board_json),

   529  function undoMove(accountId) {
   530    const current = getGameRow(accountId);
   531    const lastMove = db.prepare(`
   532      SELECT * FROM moves
   533      WHERE account_id = ? AND applied = 1
   534      ORDER BY move_number DESC
   535      LIMIT 1
   536    `).get(accountId);
   537  
   538    if (!lastMove) {
   539      return { ok: false, status: 409, message: 'Nothing to undo' };
   540    }
   541  
   542    const state = {
   543      board: JSON.parse(current.board_json),
   544      currentPlayer: current.current_player,
   545      status: current.status,
   546      winnerColor: current.winner_color,
   547      winningCells: JSON.parse(current.winning_cells_json),
   548      totals: {
   549        redWins: current.red_wins,
   550        yellowWins: current.yellow_wins,
   551        draws: current.draws,
   552      },
   553    };
   554  
   555    const index = rowColToIndex(lastMove.row_index, 
lastMove.column_index);
   556    state.board[index] = null;
   557    state.currentPlayer = lastMove.color;
   558    state.status = 'active';
   559    state.winnerColor = null;
   560    state.winningCells = [];
   561    if (current.status === 'won') {
   562      if (current.winner_color === 'Red') {
   563        state.totals.redWins -= 1;
   564      } else if (current.winner_color === 'Yellow') {
   565        state.totals.yellowWins -= 1;
   566      }
   567    } else if (current.status === 'draw') {
   568      state.totals.draws -= 1;
   569    }
   570  
   571    const run = db.transaction(() => {
   572      db.prepare('UPDATE moves SET applied = 0 WHERE account_id = ? AND 
move_number = ?').run(accountId, lastMove.move_number);
   573      db.prepare(`
   574        UPDATE game_state
   575        SET board_json = ?, current_player = ?, status = 'active', 
winner_color = NULL, winning_cells_json = ?,
   576            red_wins = ?, yellow_wins = ?, draws = ?, updated_at = 
CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
   577        WHERE account_id = ?
   578      `).run(
   579        serializeBoard(state.board),
   580        state.currentPlayer,
   581        serializeWinningCells([]),
   582        state.totals.redWins,
   583        state.totals.yellowWins,
   584        state.totals.draws,
   585        accountId,
   586      );
   587    });
   588  
   589    run();
   590    return { ok: true, feedback: '' };
   591  }

const base = 'http://127.0.0.1:3000';
async function post(path, token, body = {}) {
  const res = await fetch(base + path, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  const text = await res.text();
  const data = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${path} ${res.status}: ${data.error || text}`);
  return data;
}
async function login(email, password) {
  return post('/api/login', null, { email, password });
}
(async () => {
  const a1 = await login('avery@dropline.test', 'password123');
  let token = a1.token;
  console.log('Avery initial', a1.statusText, a1.totals.redWins, 
a1.history.length);
  for (const column of [1, 2, 1, 2, 1, 2]) {
    const r = await post('/api/move', token, { column });
    token = token || r.token;
    console.log('move', column, r.statusText, r.totals.redWins, 
r.history.length);
    token = a1.token;
  }
  const win = await post('/api/move', token, { column: 1 });
  console.log('win', win.statusText, win.totals.redWins, 
win.winningCells.length, win.history.length);
  const undo = await post('/api/undo', token, {});
  console.log('undo', undo.statusText, undo.totals.redWins, undo.canRedo, 
undo.history.length);
  const redo = await post('/api/redo', token, {});
  console.log('redo', redo.statusText, redo.totals.redWins, redo.canRedo, 
redo.history.length);

  const a2 = await login('avery@dropline.test', 'password123');
  console.log('Avery later sign-in', a2.statusText, a2.totals.redWins, 
a2.history.length);

  const j = await login('jordan@dropline.test', 'password123');
  console.log('Jordan', j.statusText, j.totals.redWins, j.history.length);

  const reset = await post('/api/new-game', a2.token, {});
  console.log('reset', reset.statusText, reset.totals.redWins, 
reset.history.length);
})();

$ node - <<'NODE'
const base='http://127.0.0.1:3000';
async function post(path, token, body={}) {
  const res = await fetch(base + path, {method:'POST', 
headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json', ...(token ? {Authorization:`Bearer 
${token}`} : {})}, body: JSON.stringify(body)});
  const data = await res.json();
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error || path);
  return data;
}
(async()=>{
  const login = await post('/api/login', null, {email:'avery@dropline.test', 
password:'password123'});
  const token = login.token;
  await post('/api/new-game', token, {});
  await post('/api/move', token, {column:1});
  await post('/api/move', token, {column:2});
  const beforeUndo = await post('/api/move', token, {column:1});
  const undo = await post('/api/undo', token, {});
  const afterNew = await post('/api/move', token, {column:3});
  console.log('beforeUndo', beforeUndo.history.map(m=>m.moveNumber).join(','), 
beforeUndo.canRedo, beforeUndo.statusText);
  console.log('undo', undo.canRedo, undo.history.map(m=>m.moveNumber).join(','),
undo.statusText);
  console.log('afterNew', afterNew.history.map(m=>m.moveNumber).join(','), 
afterNew.canRedo, afterNew.statusText);
  await post('/api/new-game', token, {});
})();
